113	    const p = Math.min(1, this.t / (this.anim / 2)); // 飞出进度
114	    const out = this.t < this.anim / 2 ? p : 1 - (this.t - this.anim / 2) / (this.anim / 2);
115	    const len = this.maxLen * Math.max(0, out);
116	    const sag = Math.sin(p * Math.PI) * 24; // 弧线下垂
117	    return {

119	      y: this.y + Math.sin(this.ang) * len + sag,
120	    };

130	    const falloff = WHIP_FALLOFF[this.projId] ?? 0.7;
131	    const baseDmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(this.damage * (owner ? owner.summonDamageMult : 1))

149	          e.whipTagDmg = tag;
150	          e.whipTagCrit = WHIP_TAG_CRIT[this.itemId] ?? 0;
151	          e.whipTagItem = this.itemId;
152	        }
153	        // proc 型鞭（:12932-12974）：命中启用 240t 一次性 proc（随从首跳触发，见 MinionProj）
154	        if (WHIP_PROC_ITEMS.has(this.itemId)) {
155	          e.whipProcT = 240;
156	          e.whipProcItem = this.itemId;

162	        const deb = e as unknown as { onFireT?: number; poisonT?: number };
163	        if (this.projId === 913 || this.projId === 912) deb.onFireT = Math.max(deb.onFireT ?? 0, 4);
164	        if (this.projId === 914 && Math.random() < 0.2) deb.poisonT = Math.max(deb.poisonT ?? 0, 3);
165	        // 玩家 buff（WhipTagEffect.PlayerBuffId：命中授予 180t）+ 附属召唤
166	        const pb = WHIP_PLAYER_BUFF[this.itemId];

180	        if (this.t >= at && this.t - 1 < at) {
181	          const lash = new WhipProj(owner.cx, owner.cy, this.ang, this.projId, this.itemId,
182	            this.damage, this.knockback, 30, this.maxLen / (this.anim * 2) * 30 * 2 > 0 ? this.maxLen / this.anim * 30 : this.maxLen,
183	            () => owner);
184	          lash.extraLash = k;
185	          (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(lash, 'projectiles');
186	        }
187	      }
188	    }
189	    // 生存期不位移（根部跟随玩家在 fixedUpdate 首段同步）
190	  }
191	
192	  /** 命中附属召唤（OnHit switch）：酷鞭 4911 → 雪花哨兵 917（伤 15，buff 312 维持）；
193	   *  麻线鞭 5473 → 小蜘蛛 1036（伤 2，buff 365 维持）——同类在场不重复生成 */
194	  private spawnSidekick(game: GameHooks, owner: Player, itemId: number): void {
195	    const want = itemId === 4911 ? 917 : itemId === 5473 ? 1036 : 0;
196	    if (!want) return;
197	    const projs = (game as unknown as { entities: { projectiles: MinionProj[]; add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities;
198	    if (projs.projectiles.some((m) => m instanceof MinionProj && !m.dead && m.projId === want)) return;
199	    const m = new MinionProj(owner.cx, owner.cy - 30, want, want === 917 ? 15 : 2, 1.5,
200	      false, 0, false, () => (owner.dead ? null : owner));
201	    m.noSlot = true;
202	    projs.add(m, 'projectiles');
203	  }
204	
205	  draw(r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer): void {
206	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
207	    if (!ctx) return;
208	    const img = projFrameImg(this.projId, 0);
209	    const tip = this.tip();
210	    if (!img) {
211	      // 兜底：细线
212	      ctx.strokeStyle = '#D8B060';
213	      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
214	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(this.x, this.y); ctx.lineTo(tip.x, tip.y); ctx.stroke();
215	      return;
216	    }
217	    ctx.save();
218	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
219	    // 沿根部→鞭尖排布 8 个贴图段（近似原版 Bezier 段绘制）
220	    const segs = 8;
221	    for (let s = 0; s < segs; s++) {
222	      const k = s / segs;
223	      const px = this.x + (tip.x - this.x) * k, py = this.y + (tip.y - this.y) * k;
224	      const a = Math.atan2(tip.y - this.y, tip.x - this.x);
225	      ctx.save();
226	      ctx.translate(px, py);
227	      ctx.rotate(a + Math.PI / 2);
228	      if (this.spriteDir < 0) ctx.scale(1, -1);
229	      ctx.drawImage(img, -img.width / 2, -img.height / 2);
230	      ctx.restore();
231	    }
232	    ctx.restore();
233	  }
234	}
235	

```

232	    this.rot = Math.atan2(par.cy - this.cy, par.cx - this.cx) + Math.PI / 2;
233	    const segN = this.peerCount(game, 626) + this.peerCount(game, 627);
234	    this.contact(game, dmg0 * (1 + 0.23 * Math.min(50, segN)), 7);
235	  }
236	
237	  /** 计数器（831 虎宝石 / 970 阿比盖尔计数，AI_164 :43946）：头顶环绕吸附、不攻击；
238	   *  派生唯一本体（档位随同类数），本体 noSlot 不占上限 */
239	  private counterHover(game: GameHooks, p: Player, dmg0: number): void {
240	    void dmg0;
241	    const projs = (game as unknown as { entities: { projectiles: MinionProj[] } }).entities.projectiles;
242	    if (this.projId === 831) {
243	      const gems = this.peerCount(game, 831);
244	      const want = gems <= 3 ? 833 : gems <= 6 ? 834 : 835; // GetDesiredStormTigerMinionRank
245	      const tigers = projs.filter((m) => m instanceof MinionProj && !m.dead && m.projId >= 833 && m.projId <= 835);
246	      if (!tigers.length) this.spawnBody(game, want, this.damage);
247	      else if (tigers[0].projId !== want) { tigers[0].dead = true; this.spawnBody(game, want, this.damage); }
248	    } else if (!projs.some((m) => m instanceof MinionProj && !m.dead && m.projId === 963)) {
249	      this.spawnBody(game, 963, this.damage);
250	    }
251	    // AI_164 环绕：每层 4 个、层高 +12px、中心头顶 -61px（MountedCenter-21-40）
252	    const peers = projs.filter((m) => m instanceof MinionProj && !m.dead && m.projId === this.projId);
253	    const idx = peers.indexOf(this);
254	    const layer = Math.floor(idx / 4), inLayer = idx % 4;
255	    const layerCount = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, peers.length - layer * 4));
256	    const ang = (this.age * 2 + layer / 8 + inLayer / layerCount) * Math.PI * 2;
257	    const r = 8 + 12 * layer;
258	    this.x = p.cx + Math.sin(ang) * r - this.w / 2;
259	    this.y = p.cy - 61 - Math.cos(ang) * r - this.h / 2;
260	    this.facing = p.facing;
261	  }
262	
263	  /** 沙漠虎本体（833/834/835，minionSlots=0）：地面追击 + 周期冲刺（818 链近似为
264	   *  直线穿墙冲刺，132 判定盒、冲刺伤 ×(1.5+0.4n)→取 1.5×缩放）；伤害 ×(1+0.4×(宝石数-1)) */
265	  private tigerBody(game: GameHooks, p: Player, dmg0: number): void {
266	    const gems = Math.max(1, this.peerCount(game, 831));
267	    const scale = 1 + 0.4 * (gems - 1);
268	    const cd = this.projId === 833 ? 360 : this.projId === 834 ? 300 : 240;
269	    const tgt = this.nearestEnemy(game, 800);
270	    if (this.dashT > 0) {
271	      this.dashT--;
272	      if (tgt) {
273	        const d = Math.hypot(tgt.cx - this.cx, tgt.cy - this.cy) || 1;
274	        this.x += ((tgt.cx - this.cx) / d) * 12;
275	        this.y += ((tgt.cy - this.cy) / d) * 12;
276	      }
277	      // 818 冲刺伤 ×(1.5+0.4×(宝石数-1))（:12108）
278	      this.contact(game, dmg0 * scale * (1.5 + 0.4 * (gems - 1)), 10, 58);
279	      return;
280	    }
281	    if (this.pounceCd > 0) this.pounceCd--;
282	    if (tgt && this.pounceCd <= 0) { this.dashT = 30; this.pounceCd = cd; return; }
283	    // 地面追击（AI_067 地面段近似）
284	    if (tgt) {
285	      this.vx += Math.sign(tgt.cx - this.cx) * 0.12;
286	      this.vx = Math.max(-3.5, Math.min(3.5, this.vx));
287	      if (this.onGround && tgt.cy < this.cy - 24 && Math.random() < 0.05) this.vy = -6;
288	      this.facing = Math.sign(tgt.cx - this.cx) || this.facing;
289	    } else {
290	      this.vx *= 0.9;
291	      const dx = p.cx - this.cx;
292	      if (Math.abs(dx) > 60) this.vx = Math.max(-2.5, Math.min(2.5, dx * 0.02));
293	      this.facing = Math.sign(dx) || p.facing;
294	    }
295	    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
296	    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
297	    this.contact(game, dmg0 * scale, 10);
298	  }
299	
300	  /** 阿比盖尔本体 963（aiStyle 62 前段 :62109-63100）：飞行近战，<50px 挥击
301	   *  （20t/敌）；伤害 ×(1+0.55×(n-1))（肉前档，1.4.5 困难模式 1.3） */
302	  private abigailBody(game: GameHooks, p: Player, dmg0: number): void {
303	    const n = Math.max(1, this.peerCount(game, 970));
304	    const scale = 1 + 0.55 * (n - 1);
305	    const tgt = this.nearestEnemy(game, 700);
306	    if (tgt) {
307	      const d = Math.hypot(tgt.cx - this.cx, tgt.cy - this.cy) || 1;
308	      const spd = d > 200 ? 7.4 : d > 50 ? 5.4 : 0; // 6+1.4n / 4+1.4n（n=1 档）
309	      this.vx = (tgt.cx - this.cx) / d * spd;
310	      this.vy = (tgt.cy - this.cy) / d * spd;
311	      if (d < 50) this.attackFlash = 24;
312	      this.facing = Math.sign(tgt.cx - this.cx) || this.facing;
313	    } else {
314	      const hx = p.cx - p.facing * 40, hy = p.cy + 40;
315	      const dx = hx - this.cx, dy = hy - this.cy;
316	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
317	      const spd = Math.min(4.8, d * 0.08);
318	      this.vx = (dx / d) * spd;
319	      this.vy = (dy / d) * spd;
320	      this.facing = p.facing;
321	    }
322	    this.x += this.vx;
323	    this.y += this.vy;
324	    this.contact(game, dmg0 * scale, 20);
325	  }

327	  /** 星尘守护者 623（套装奖励，Player.cs:16195）：站玩家侧后 -(5+w/2)×dir,-25；
328	   *  500 内最近敌瞬移冲刺拳击（AI_120 近似） */
329	  private guardianBody(game: GameHooks, p: Player, dmg0: number): void {
330	    const tgt = this.nearestEnemy(game, 500);
331	    if (tgt) {
332	      const d = Math.hypot(tgt.cx - this.cx, tgt.cy - this.cy) || 1;
333	      this.vx = (tgt.cx - this.cx) / d * 10;
334	      this.vy = (tgt.cy - this.cy) / d * 10;
335	      this.facing = Math.sign(tgt.cx - this.cx) || this.facing;
336	    } else {
337	      const hx = p.cx - p.facing * (5 + this.w / 2), hy = p.cy - 25;
338	      const dx = hx - this.cx, dy = hy - this.cy;
339	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
340	      const spd = Math.min(10, d * 0.1);
341	      this.vx = (dx / d) * spd;
342	      this.vy = (dy / d) * spd;
343	      this.facing = p.facing;
344	    }
345	    this.x += this.vx;
346	    this.y += this.vy;
347	    this.contact(game, dmg0, 10);
348	  }

350	  /** 接触伤害通用（pad=判定盒外扩，818 冲刺用）；鞭 tag + TagCrit 语义同主线 */
351	  private contact(game: GameHooks, dmg: number, cd: number, pad = 0): void {
352	    const add = (e: unknown, b: string) =>
353	      (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (x: unknown, y: string) => void } }).entities.add(e, b);
354	    for (const e of game.enemies()) {
355	      const en = e as never as EnemyLike & {
356	        dead: boolean; whipTagT?: number; whipTagDmg?: number; whipTagCrit?: number;
357	        whipTagItem?: number; whipProcT?: number; whipProcItem?: number;
358	      };
359	      if (en.dead) continue;
360	      if (!(this.x - pad < en.x + en.w && this.x + this.w + pad > en.x
361	        && this.y - pad < en.y + en.h && this.y + this.h + pad > en.y)) continue;
362	      if (!this.hitCd.ready(en.id, cd)) continue;
363	      const tag = en.whipTagT && en.whipTagT > 0 ? (en.whipTagDmg ?? 0) : 0;
364	      const crit = tag > 0 && Math.random() * 100 < (en.whipTagCrit ?? 0);
365	      let dmg1 = (dmg + tag) * (crit ? 2 : 1);
366	      // 黑收成 OnTaggedHit（WhipTagEffect_DarkHarvest :9-20：每次 tag 命中 → 916 黑闪电，
367	      // 伤害 = TagDamage×乘率，armorPen 50）——视觉走 916 弹幕
368	      if (tag > 0 && en.whipTagItem === 4680) {
369	        en.hurt(Math.max(1, Math.round(tag)), Math.sign(en.cx - this.cx), -1, game);
370	        add(new Arrow(en.cx, en.cy, 0, -1, 0, 0, 916, null, { grav: 0, life: 24, pierce: 99 }), 'projectiles');
371	        game.spawnParticles(en.cx, en.cy, '#7A3AFF', 6, 2.2, { life: 20 });
372	      }
373	      // proc 型鞭首跳（TagEffectState：ModifyProcHit→OnProcHit 一次性）：
374	      //  4912 火鞭 ×2.75 + 918 爆炸（伤×1.75,96 盒）；5476 星陨（×1.33 自上而下）；
375	      //  5477 花瓣×3（各 40）；5479 星座星（×0.33 若干）；5480 月主弹（20，3 连近似 1 发）
376	      if (en.whipProcT && en.whipProcT > 0 && en.whipProcItem) {
377	        const pi = en.whipProcItem;
378	        en.whipProcT = 0;
379	        if (pi === 4912) {
380	          dmg1 *= 2.75; // ProcDamageMultiplier 1.75 → ModifyProcHit 合计 ×2.75
381	          const ex = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg * 1.75));
382	          for (const ent2 of game.enemies()) {
383	            const en2 = ent2 as EnemyLike & { dead: boolean };
384	            if (en2.dead || Math.abs(en2.cx - en.cx) > 48 + en2.w / 2 || Math.abs(en2.cy - en.cy) > 48 + en2.h / 2) continue;
385	            en2.hurt(ex, Math.sign(en2.cx - en.cx) * 2, -2, game);
386	          }
387	          game.spawnParticles(en.cx, en.cy, '#FF7A28', 16, 4, { life: 26 });
388	        } else if (pi === 5476) {
389	          add(new Arrow(en.cx - 6, en.cy - 280, 0.4, 8, Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg * 1.33)), 2, 1037, null, { grav: 0 }), 'projectiles');
390	        } else if (pi === 5477) {
391	          for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
392	            const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
393	            add(new Arrow(en.cx, en.cy, Math.cos(a) * 3.5, Math.sin(a) * 3.5, 40, 2, 1038, null, { grav: 0, life: 120 }), 'projectiles');
394	          }
395	        } else if (pi === 5479) {
396	          for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
397	            add(new Arrow(en.cx, en.cy - 20 - i * 30, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2, 3,
398	              Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg * 0.33)), 1, 1039, null, { grav: 0, life: 240 }), 'projectiles');
399	          }
400	        } else if (pi === 5480) {
401	          add(new Arrow(en.cx, en.cy - 30, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, 6, 20, 1, 1045, null, { grav: 0 }), 'projectiles');
402	        }
403	      }
404	      en.hurt(Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg1)), Math.sign(en.cx - this.cx) * this.knockback * 0.5, -1, game);
405	      this.attackFlash = 24;
406	    }
407	  }

409	  /** 同类计数（计数器/本体伤害档位用：831 宝石数 / 970 阿比盖尔数） */
410	  private peerCount(game: GameHooks, id: number): number {
411	    let n = 0;
412	    for (const e of (game as unknown as { entities: { projectiles: unknown[] } }).entities.projectiles) {
413	      const m = e as MinionProj;
414	      if (m instanceof MinionProj && !m.dead && m.projId === id) n++;
415	    }
416	    return n;
417	  }
418	
419	  /** 生成不占槽实体（计数器派生本体/守护者） */
420	  private spawnBody(game: GameHooks, projId: number, damage: number): MinionProj {
421	    const owner = this.ownerRef();
422	    const m = new MinionProj(this.cx, this.cy, projId, damage, this.knockback,
423	      false, 0, false, () => owner);
424	    m.noSlot = true;
425	    (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(m, 'projectiles');
426	    return m;
427	  }
428	
429	  constructor(
430	    x: number, y: number, projId: number, damage: number, knockback: number,
431	    sentry: boolean, slot: number, ground: boolean,
432	    owner: () => Player | null,
433	  ) {
434	    super();
435	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
436	    this.projId = projId;
437	    this.damage = damage;
438	    this.knockback = knockback;
439	    this.sentry = sentry;
440	    this.slot = slot;
441	    this.ground = ground;
442	    this.life = sentry ? 60 * 600 : Infinity; // 哨兵 10 分钟（哨兵类默认时限近似）
443	    this.ownerRef = owner;
444	  }
445	
446	  /** 最近敌人（无目标 null）。鞭 tag 优先（原版随从锁定被鞭标记目标：
447	   *  Projectile.ApplyTag → npc.whipSet/whipTime，随从 TargetSlot 优先取 tag 敌） */
448	  private nearestEnemy(game: GameHooks, maxRange: number): EnemyLike | null {
449	    const p = this.ownerRef();
450	    if (!p) return null;
451	    let best: EnemyLike | null = null;
452	    let bestD = maxRange;
453	    let tagged: EnemyLike | null = null;
454	    let taggedD = maxRange;
455	    for (const e of game.enemies()) {
456	      const en = e as never as EnemyLike & { dead: boolean; whipTagT?: number };
457	      if (!en || en.dead) continue;
458	      const d = Math.hypot(en.cx - this.cx, en.cy - this.cy);
459	      if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; best = en; }
460	      if (en.whipTagT && en.whipTagT > 0 && d < taggedD) { taggedD = d; tagged = en; }
461	    }
462	    // 右键集火优先（OwnerMinionAttackTargetNPC 模板 :27431：有效且距本随从 <900）
463	    const focus = (p as unknown as { minionTargetId?: number }).minionTargetId ?? -1;
464	    if (focus >= 0) {
465	      for (const e of game.enemies()) {
466	        const en = e as never as EnemyLike;
467	        if (!en.dead && en.id === focus && Math.hypot(en.cx - this.cx, en.cy - this.cy) < 900) return en;
468	      }
469	    }
470	    return tagged ?? best;
471	  }
472	
473	  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
474	    void dt;
475	    this.age++;
476	    if (this.attackFlash > 0) this.attackFlash--;
477	    if (this.sentry) {
478	      if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
479	      // 落地（AI_130 尾部 :65420：tileCollide=true + velocity.Y += 0.2）
480	      this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + 0.2, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
481	      moveAndCollide(this, game.world, 0, this.vy);
482	      const owner = this.ownerRef();
483	      // 随从/哨兵伤害：武器基伤 × summonDamageMult（live 刷新，换装即时生效，
484	      // Projectile.cs:15368 语义）+ 护甲穿透（对随从弹幕生效 :12068）
485	      const dmgOf = () => Math.max(1, Math.round(this.damage * (owner ? owner.summonDamageMult : 1))
486	        + (owner ? owner.equipStats.armorPen : 0));
487	      // 爆炸烈焰塔族（AI_130，Projectile.cs:65194-65450 1:1）：
488	      // 待机帧 0 → 锁定目标(900px 内)转向 → 开火动画帧 1-6 每 4t（第 12t 从炮口
489	      // Bottom+(dir*6,-46) 发火球 668）→ 24t 结束回待机并冷却 60t（num8）
490	      if (FLAMEBURST_TOWER.has(this.projId)) {
491	        if (this.sFire === 0) {
492	          const tgt = this.nearestEnemy(game, 900);
493	          if (tgt && this.sCool <= 0) {
494	            this.facing = tgt.cx >= this.cx ? 1 : -1; // direction = Sign(指向)（:65386）
495	            this.sFire = 1; this.sFireT = 0;
496	          }
497	          if (this.sCool > 0) this.sCool--;
498	        } else {
499	          if (this.sFireT === 12) {
500	            const tgt = this.nearestEnemy(game, 900);
501	            game.playSfx('dd2Flameburst', 0.9, this.cx, this.cy); // AI_131 :65533（弹幕 localAI[0]==0）
502	            const mx = this.cx + this.facing * 6, my = this.y + this.h - 46; // 炮口（:65316）
503	            let vx = this.facing * 9, vy = 0;
504	            if (tgt) {
505	              const d = Math.hypot(tgt.cx - mx, tgt.cy - my) || 1;
506	              vx = ((tgt.cx - mx) / d) * 9; vy = ((tgt.cy - my) / d) * 9;
507	            }
508	            (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(
509	              Object.assign(new Arrow(mx - 5, my - 5, vx, vy, dmgOf(), this.knockback, 668, null, { grav: 0.2 }), { whipTagShot: true }),
510	              'projectiles');
511	          }
512	          if (++this.sFireT >= 24) { this.sFire = 0; this.sCool = 60; } // num6*num7=24、num8=60
513	        }
514	        return;
515	      }
516	      // 弩车族（AI_134）：帧 0 待机 → 帧表 1-5（每 5t）→ 第 12t 弩箭 680（速 16）
517	      // → 25t 结束 → 冷却 160（GetBallistraShotDelay）
518	      if (BALLISTA_TOWER.has(this.projId)) {
519	        if (this.sFire === 0) {
520	          const tgt = this.nearestEnemy(game, 900);
521	          if (tgt) this.facing = tgt.cx >= this.cx ? 1 : -1;
522	          if (tgt && this.sCool <= 0) { this.sFire = 1; this.sFireT = 0; }
523	          if (this.sCool > 0) this.sCool--;
524	        } else {
525	          if (this.sFireT === 12) {
526	            const tgt = this.nearestEnemy(game, 900);
527	            game.playSfx('dd2Ballista', 0.9, this.cx, this.cy); // AI_134 :65674（开火帧同弩箭）
528	            let vx = this.facing * 16, vy = 0;
529	            if (tgt) {
530	              const d = Math.hypot(tgt.cx - this.cx, tgt.cy - (this.y + 20)) || 1;
531	              vx = ((tgt.cx - this.cx) / d) * 16; vy = ((tgt.cy - (this.y + 20)) / d) * 16;
532	            }
533	            (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(
534	              Object.assign(new Arrow(this.cx, this.y + 20, vx, vy, dmgOf(), this.knockback, 680, null, { grav: 0 }), { whipTagShot: true }),
535	              'projectiles');
536	          }
537	          if (++this.sFireT >= 25) { this.sFire = 0; this.sCool = 160; }
538	        }
539	        return;
540	      }
541	      // 闪电光环族（AI_137）：非射击——每 3t 对塔周 4 格盒内敌人场伤
542	      // （localNPCHitCooldown=3；宽 999 扫描增长近似为固定 4 格盒）
543	      if (AURA_TOWER.has(this.projId)) {
544	        if (--this.sCool <= 0) {
545	          this.sCool = 3;
546	          for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
547	            const en = ent as EnemyLike & { dead: boolean };
548	            if (en.dead) continue;
549	            if (Math.abs(en.cx - this.cx) > TILE * 4 || Math.abs(en.cy - this.cy) > TILE * 4) continue;
550	            if (!this.hitCd.ready(en.id, 3)) continue;
551	            // SummonTagDamageMultiplier（ProjectileID.cs:31）：闪电 aura 族 tag 伤减半
552	            const tagA = (en as { whipTagT?: number; whipTagDmg?: number }).whipTagT ? Math.round(((en as { whipTagDmg?: number }).whipTagDmg ?? 0) * 0.5) : 0;
553	            en.hurt(dmgOf() + tagA, 0, 0, game);
554	          }
555	        }
556	        return;
557	      }
558	      // 爆炸机关族（AI_138）：每 3t 探测中心上方 48px 的 144×144 盒——有敌即
559	      // 原地爆炸 AoE（proj 694 等价）+ 粒子 + 冷却 90（GetExplosiveTrapCooldown）
560	      if (TRAP_TOWER.has(this.projId)) {
561	        if (--this.sCool <= 0) {
562	          this.sCool = 3;
563	          const ecx = this.cx, ecy = this.cy - 48;
564	          const hit = (game.enemies() as EnemyLike[]).some((en) =>
565	            !en.dead && Math.abs(en.cx - ecx) < 72 + en.w / 2 && Math.abs(en.cy - ecy) < 72 + en.h / 2);
566	          if (hit) {
567	            this.sCool = 90;
568	            game.playSfx('dd2Explosive', 0.9); // AI_138 :66104（无位置参数）
569	            let boom = 0;
570	            for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
571	              const en = ent as EnemyLike & { dead: boolean };
572	              if (en.dead) continue;
573	              if (Math.abs(en.cx - ecx) < 72 + en.w / 2 && Math.abs(en.cy - ecy) < 72 + en.h / 2) {
574	                en.hurt(dmgOf(), Math.sign(en.cx - this.cx) * this.knockback, -2, game);
575	                boom++;
576	              }
577	            }
578	            void boom;
579	            game.spawnParticles(ecx, ecy, '#FFB040', 18, 4, { life: 36 });
580	            game.playSfx('explode', 0.8, this.cx, this.cy);
581	          }
582	        }
583	        return;
584	      }

          () => this.input.mouseDown && !this.input.uiBlocking && this.player.inv.heldItem()?.id === itemId,
          () => { const [wx, wy] = this.camera.screenToWorld(inp.mouseX, inp.mouseY); return { x: wx, y: wy }; },
          flail);
        // 配重球（counterWeight：悠悠球命中时落配重投射物，原版环绕弹的直线坠落近似）
        if (this.player.equipStats.counterWeight) {
          (yoyo as unknown as { spawnWeight?: (x: number, y: number, dmg: number) => void }).spawnWeight =
            (wx, wy, wdmg) => {
              this.entities.add(new Arrow(wx, wy, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2, -1.5,
                wdmg, 2, 556 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 6), null), 'projectiles');
            };
        }
        this.entities.add(yoyo, 'projectiles');

```

  const m = key.match(/^vi_(\d+)_/);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : -1;
}

export type CombatWeapon =
  | { kind: 'melee'; damage: number; knockback: number; useTime: number; useAnimation: number; autoReuse: boolean; shoot?: number; shootSpeed?: number }
  | { kind: 'summon'; shoot: number; sentry: boolean; damage: number; knockback: number; useTime: number; shootSpeed: number }
  | { kind: 'whip'; shoot: number; damage: number; knockback: number; useTime: number; shootSpeed: number; itemId: number }
  | {
      kind: 'boomerang' | 'spear' | 'yoyo' | 'flail' | 'grenade' | 'glowstick' | 'magic' | 'shot' | 'summon';
      shoot: number;
      damage: number;
      knockback: number;
      useTime: number;
      shootSpeed: number;
      mana?: number;
      sentry?: boolean;
    };

/** 物品定义的最小形状（items.ts 的 ItemDef 满足之） */
export interface CombatWeaponItemLike {
  key: string;
  vid?: number;
}

/** vi_* 物品的原版战斗语义：按 itemCombat 字段 + 投射物 aiStyle 家族分流
 *  （Projectile.cs SetDefaults 数据 + DefaultTo* 族 aiStyle）。
 *  返回 null = 无战斗语义（材料/家具/药水等，或走既有手写分支的弓）。
 *  注意：消耗型 aiStyle 2（手里剑等抛物线投掷）仍走 Game.thrownCombat，
 *  这里只接管爆炸物族（ai16）——判定顺序见 Game.useItem */
export function combatWeapon(def: CombatWeaponItemLike): CombatWeapon | null {
  const vid = def.vid ?? viIdFromKey(def.key);
  if (vid < 0) return null;
  // 鞭（DefaultToWhip，Item.cs:47439——提取器漏提，独立表 src/data/vanilla-whips.json 全 18 把）
  const whip = WHIPS[String(vid)];
  if (whip) {
    return {
      kind: 'whip', itemId: vid,
      shoot: whip.proj, damage: whip.dmg, knockback: whip.kb,
      useTime: whip.anim, shootSpeed: whip.speed,
    };
  }
  const c = itemCombat(vid);
  if (!c || c.useAmmo || c.ammo) return null; // 弓弩/弹药体系不在此分流
  const shoot = c.shoot ?? 0;
  const ai = shoot ? projectileData(shoot)?.aiStyle ?? -1 : -1;
  const base = {
    damage: c.damage ?? 1,
    knockback: c.knockBack ?? 3,
    useTime: c.useTime ?? c.useAnimation ?? 20,
    shootSpeed: c.shootSpeed ?? 8,
  };
  if (shoot) {
    if (c.consumable && c.noMelee) {
      // 消耗型：荧光棒族（282 普通/286 粘性/3112 弹力/3002 矿工/4776 妖精/5643 彩虹
      // → proj 50/53/515/473/870/1089，ai14 弹跳物理）；爆炸物族（ai16）弹跳+引信；
      // ai2 抛物线投掷武器（手里剑）交回 thrownCombat
      // 门禁 = ItemID.Sets.Glowsticks（ItemID.cs:1198 精确六件）——ai14 还有巨石/药瓶等
      // 其他投掷物，不能按 aiStyle 一律归入
      if (ai === AI_GLOWSTICK && (vid === 282 || vid === 286 || vid === 3112 || vid === 3002 || vid === 4776 || vid === 5643)) {
        return { kind: 'glowstick', shoot, ...base };
      }
      if (ai === AI_GRENADE) return { kind: 'grenade', shoot, ...base };
      return null;
    }
    if (ai === AI_BOOMERANG) return { kind: 'boomerang', shoot, ...base };
    if (ai === AI_SPEAR || ai === AI_SHORTSWORD || ai === AI_ANCHORED) return { kind: 'spear', shoot, ...base };
    // AI_ANCHORED(13):鱼叉 160/Golem拳 1297/KO加农 1314/链刀 1325/链斩 3012——
    // 原版锚在角色上伸缩(AI_013),归长矛前刺族近似;此前落入 shot 兜底=子弹射飞
    if (ai === AI_YOYO) return { kind: 'yoyo', shoot, ...base };
    if (c.summon) {
      // 召唤武器（31 件）：按投射物 AI 分流哨兵（53/54/123 定点炮台）与随从（其余飞行/地面）
      return { kind: 'summon', shoot, sentry: ai === 53 || ai === 54 || ai === 123 || ai === 130 || ai === 134 || ai === 137 || ai === 138, // OOA 四塔(aiStyle 130/134/137/138)是哨兵——曾漏标走随从路径
        ...base };
    }
    if (ai === AI_FLAIL) return { kind: 'flail', shoot, ...base };
    // ★ melee+shoot（附魔剑 989/波刃 190 等 46 件）：原版语义 = 挥砍为主 +
    // 挥击中射弹（Player.cs:42880：flag4 = itemAnimation>0 && ItemTimeIsZero →
    // ItemCheck_Shoot，useTime 节奏发弹）。此前落入 shot 兜底 = 变成"光束枪"
    // 完全不挥砍——语义错误。数值取 melee 分支，附带 shoot/shootSpeed 给挥击侧发射
    if (c.melee && !c.noMelee) {
      return {
        kind: 'melee', damage: base.damage, knockback: base.knockback, useTime: base.useTime,
        useAnimation: c.useAnimation ?? base.useTime, autoReuse: !!c.autoReuse,
        shoot, shootSpeed: base.shootSpeed,
      };
    }
    if (c.magic || c.mana) return { kind: 'magic', shoot, mana: c.mana ?? 0, ...base };
    // 其余 melee/ranged+shoot（附魔剑光束等）：直射弹兜底
    if (c.melee || c.ranged || c.noMelee) return { kind: 'shot', shoot, ...base };
    return null;

```

using System;
using System.IO;
using Terraria.ID;
using Terraria.Net;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.Items;

public class TagEffectState
{
	public class NetModule : Terraria.Net.NetModule
	{
		private enum MessageType
		{
			FullState,
			ChangeActiveEffect,
			ApplyTagToNPC,
			EnableProcOnNPC,
			ClearProcOnNPC
		}

		public static void WriteSparseNPCTimeArray(BinaryWriter writer, int[] array)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				int num = array[i];
				if (num != 0)
				{
					writer.Write((byte)i);
					writer.Write(num);
				}
			}
			writer.Write((byte)array.Length);
		}

		public static void ReadSparseNPCTimeArray(BinaryReader reader, int[] array)
		{
			Array.Clear(array, 0, array.Length);
			while (true)
			{
				int num = reader.ReadByte();
				if (num < array.Length)
				{
					array[num] = reader.ReadInt32();
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		public static NetPacket WriteFullState(TagEffectState state)
		{
			NetPacket result = Terraria.Net.NetModule.CreatePacket<NetModule>();
			result.Writer.Write((byte)state._owner.whoAmI);
			result.Writer.Write((byte)0);
			result.Writer.Write((short)state.Type);
			WriteSparseNPCTimeArray(result.Writer, state.TimeLeftOnNPC);
			if (state._effect.SyncProcs)
			{
				WriteSparseNPCTimeArray(result.Writer, state.ProcTimeLeftOnNPC);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static NetPacket WriteChangeActiveEffect(TagEffectState state)
		{
			NetPacket result = Terraria.Net.NetModule.CreatePacket<NetModule>();
			result.Writer.Write((byte)state._owner.whoAmI);
			result.Writer.Write((byte)1);
			result.Writer.Write((short)state.Type);
			return result;
		}

		private static NetPacket WriteNPCChange(TagEffectState state, MessageType msgType, int npcIndex)
		{
			NetPacket result = Terraria.Net.NetModule.CreatePacket<NetModule>();
			result.Writer.Write((byte)state._owner.whoAmI);
			result.Writer.Write((byte)msgType);
			result.Writer.Write((byte)npcIndex);
			return result;
		}

		public static NetPacket WriteApplyTagToNPC(TagEffectState state, int npcIndex)
		{
			return WriteNPCChange(state, MessageType.ApplyTagToNPC, npcIndex);
		}

		public static NetPacket WriteEnableProcOnNPC(TagEffectState state, int npcIndex)
		{
			return WriteNPCChange(state, MessageType.EnableProcOnNPC, npcIndex);
		}

		public static NetPacket WriteClearProcOnNPC(TagEffectState state, int npcIndex)
		{
			return WriteNPCChange(state, MessageType.ClearProcOnNPC, npcIndex);
		}

		public override bool Deserialize(BinaryReader reader, int userId)
		{
			int num = reader.ReadByte();
			if (Main.netMode == 2)
			{
				num = userId;
			}
			TagEffectState tagEffectState = Main.player[num].TagEffectState;
			MessageType messageType = (MessageType)reader.ReadByte();
			switch (messageType)
			{
			case MessageType.FullState:
				if (Main.netMode == 2)
				{
					return false;
				}
				tagEffectState.TrySetActiveEffect(reader.ReadInt16());
				ReadSparseNPCTimeArray(reader, tagEffectState.TimeLeftOnNPC);
				if (tagEffectState._effect.SyncProcs)
				{
					ReadSparseNPCTimeArray(reader, tagEffectState.ProcTimeLeftOnNPC);
				}
				break;
			case MessageType.ChangeActiveEffect:
				tagEffectState.TrySetActiveEffect(reader.ReadInt16());
				if (Main.netMode == 2)
				{
					NetManager.Instance.Broadcast(WriteChangeActiveEffect(tagEffectState), num);
				}
				break;
			case MessageType.ApplyTagToNPC:
			case MessageType.EnableProcOnNPC:
			case MessageType.ClearProcOnNPC:
			{
				int num2 = reader.ReadByte();
				switch (messageType)
				{
				case MessageType.ApplyTagToNPC:
					tagEffectState.ApplyTagToNPC(Main.npc[num2]);
					break;
				case MessageType.EnableProcOnNPC:
					tagEffectState.EnableProcOnNPC(Main.npc[num2]);
					break;
				case MessageType.ClearProcOnNPC:
					tagEffectState.ClearProcOnNPC(num2);
					break;
				}
				if (Main.netMode == 2)
				{
					NetManager.Instance.Broadcast(WriteNPCChange(tagEffectState, messageType, num2), num);
				}
				break;
			}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static void SyncStateIfNecessary(TagEffectState state, int toClient, int ignoreClient)
		{
			if (state._effect != null && state._effect.NetSync)
			{
				NetPacket packet = WriteFullState(state);
				if (toClient >= 0)
				{
					NetManager.Instance.SendToClient(packet, toClient);
				}
				else
				{
					NetManager.Instance.Broadcast(packet, ignoreClient);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private readonly Player _owner;

	private UniqueTagEffect _effect;

	private readonly int[] TimeLeftOnNPC = new int[Main.maxNPCs];

	private readonly int[] ProcTimeLeftOnNPC = new int[Main.maxNPCs];

	public int Type { get; private set; }

	public TagEffectState(Player owner)
	{
		_owner = owner;
	}

	public bool IsNPCTagged(int npcIndex)
	{
		return TimeLeftOnNPC[npcIndex] > 0;
	}

	public bool CanProcOnNPC(int npcIndex)
	{
		return ProcTimeLeftOnNPC[npcIndex] > 0;
	}

	public void ClearProcOnNPC(int npcIndex)
	{
		ProcTimeLeftOnNPC[npcIndex] = 0;
		if (_effect.NetSync && _owner == Main.LocalPlayer)
		{
			NetManager.Instance.SendToServer(NetModule.WriteClearProcOnNPC(this, npcIndex));
		}
	}

	public void ResetNPCSlotData(int npcIndex)
	{
		TimeLeftOnNPC[npcIndex] = 0;
		ProcTimeLeftOnNPC[npcIndex] = 0;
	}

	private void ApplyTagToNPC(NPC npc)
	{
		if (_effect != null)
		{
			TimeLeftOnNPC[npc.whoAmI] = _effect.TagDuration;
			if (_effect.NetSync && _owner == Main.LocalPlayer)
			{
				NetManager.Instance.SendToServer(NetModule.WriteApplyTagToNPC(this, npc.whoAmI));
			}
			_effect.OnTagAppliedToNPC(_owner, npc);
		}
	}

	private void EnableProcOnNPC(NPC npc)
	{
		if (_effect != null)
		{
			ProcTimeLeftOnNPC[npc.whoAmI] = _effect.TagDuration;
			if (_effect.NetSync && _owner == Main.LocalPlayer)
			{
				NetManager.Instance.SendToServer(NetModule.WriteEnableProcOnNPC(this, npc.whoAmI));
			}
		}
	}

	public void Update()
	{
		if (_effect == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < TimeLeftOnNPC.Length; i++)
		{
			if (TimeLeftOnNPC[i] > 0)
			{
				TimeLeftOnNPC[i]--;
			}
		}
		for (int j = 0; j < ProcTimeLeftOnNPC.Length; j++)
		{
			if (ProcTimeLeftOnNPC[j] > 0)
			{
				ProcTimeLeftOnNPC[j]--;
			}
		}
	}

	private void Clear()
	{
		Array.Clear(TimeLeftOnNPC, 0, TimeLeftOnNPC.Length);
		Array.Clear(ProcTimeLeftOnNPC, 0, ProcTimeLeftOnNPC.Length);
	}

	public void TryApplyTagToNPC(int itemType, NPC npc)
	{
		if (ItemID.Sets.UniqueTagEffects[itemType].CanApplyTagToNPC(npc.type))
		{
			TrySetActiveEffect(itemType);
			ApplyTagToNPC(npc);
		}
	}

	public void TryEnableProcOnNPC(int expectedActiveEffectType, NPC npc)
	{
		if (Type == expectedActiveEffectType)
		{
			EnableProcOnNPC(npc);
		}
	}

	public void TrySetActiveEffect(int type)
	{
		if (Type != type)
		{
			if (_effect != null)
			{
				_effect.OnRemovedFromPlayer(_owner);
			}
			Clear();
			UniqueTagEffect effect = _effect;
			Type = type;
			_effect = ItemID.Sets.UniqueTagEffects[type];
			if (_owner == Main.LocalPlayer && ((_effect != null && _effect.NetSync) || (effect != null && effect.NetSync)))
			{
				NetManager.Instance.SendToServer(NetModule.WriteChangeActiveEffect(this));
			}
			if (_effect != null)
			{
				_effect.OnSetToPlayer(_owner);
			}
		}
	}

	public void ModifyHit(Projectile optionalProjectile, NPC npcHit, ref int damageDealt, ref bool crit)
	{
		if (_effect != null && IsNPCTagged(npcHit.whoAmI) && _effect.CanRunHitEffects(_owner, optionalProjectile, npcHit))
		{
			_effect.ModifyTaggedHit(_owner, optionalProjectile, npcHit, ref damageDealt, ref crit);
			if (CanProcOnNPC(npcHit.whoAmI))
			{
				_effect.ModifyProcHit(_owner, optionalProjectile, npcHit, ref damageDealt, ref crit);
			}
		}
	}

	public void OnHit(Projectile optionalProjectile, NPC npcHit, int calcDamage)
	{
		if (_effect != null && IsNPCTagged(npcHit.whoAmI) && _effect.CanRunHitEffects(_owner, optionalProjectile, npcHit))
		{
			_effect.OnTaggedHit(_owner, optionalProjectile, npcHit, calcDamage);
			if (CanProcOnNPC(npcHit.whoAmI))
			{
				ClearProcOnNPC(npcHit.whoAmI);
				_effect.OnProcHit(_owner, optionalProjectile, npcHit, calcDamage);
			}
		}
	}
}
using Terraria.ID;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.Items;

public class WhipTagEffect : UniqueTagEffect
{
	public int PlayerBuffId;

	public int PlayerBuffTime;

	public bool PlayerBuffAppliedManually;

	public int CritChance;

	public int TagDamage;

	private const int generalWhipMarkDuration = 240;

	public WhipTagEffect()
	{
		TagDuration = 240;
	}

	public override bool CanApplyTagToNPC(int npcType)
	{
		if (!NPCID.Sets.DebuffImmunitySets.TryGetValue(npcType, out var value))
		{
			return true;
		}
		if (value != null)
		{
			return !value.ImmuneToWhips;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public override void OnRemovedFromPlayer(Player player)
	{
		if (player == Main.LocalPlayer)
		{
			player.ClearBuff(PlayerBuffId);
		}
	}

	public override void OnTagAppliedToNPC(Player player, NPC npc)
	{
		if (player == Main.LocalPlayer)
		{
			AddTheBuff(player);
		}
	}

	protected void AddTheBuff(Player player)
	{
		if (!PlayerBuffAppliedManually && PlayerBuffId > 0)
		{
			player.AddBuff(PlayerBuffId, PlayerBuffTime);
		}
	}

	public override void ModifyTaggedHit(Player owner, Projectile optionalProjectile, NPC npcHit, ref int damageDealt, ref bool crit)
	{
		if (optionalProjectile != null)
		{
			damageDealt += (int)((float)(TagDamage + optionalProjectile.bonusTagDamage) * ProjectileID.Sets.SummonTagDamageMultiplier[optionalProjectile.type]);
		}
		if (Main.rand.Next(100) < CritChance)
		{
			crit = true;
		}
	}

	public override bool CanRunHitEffects(Player owner, Projectile optionalProjectile, NPC npcHit)
	{
		if (optionalProjectile == null || !optionalProjectile.OwnedBySomeone)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!optionalProjectile.minion && !ProjectileID.Sets.MinionShot[optionalProjectile.type] && !optionalProjectile.sentry)
		{
			return ProjectileID.Sets.SentryShot[optionalProjectile.type];
		}
		return true;
	}
}

  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：对敌判定归拥有者客户端；对玩家（hostile）由访客本地结算 Damage_EVP（netPuppetProjContact）
    if (this.netPuppet) { this.netPuppetStep(); return; }
    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.killNow(game); return; }
    const world = game.world;
    // aiStyle 1/2 通用重力（原版箭 0.3/tick 抛物线；直飞弹 grav=0）
    if (this.grav !== 0) this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + this.grav, 16);
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
    if (!world.store.inBounds(tx, ty)) { this.killNow(game); return; }
    const tileType = world.store.get(tx, ty);
    if (tileType !== 0) {
      // 可砍物（杂草/瓦罐）：Projectile.CutTiles 语义——弹幕扫过即砍
      game.cutTile(tx, ty);
      // 阻挡判定只看【实心】(Main.tileSolid 语义):树干/火把/平台等非实心格
      // 箭直接穿过(此前 tileType!==0 一刀切,箭会被树挡住——树 solid:false)
      if (!world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) return;
      // aiStyle 14 弹跳弹（希腊火 326-328 / 装饰球 346）：撞块法向反弹不消亡
      if (this.bounce && this.bounceOff(game)) return;
      // 实心块：1/3 概率回收掉落（原版箭 Kill 的掉落），然后消失
      if (this.dropKey && Math.random() < 1 / 3) game.spawnDrop(this.x, this.y, this.dropKey, 1);
      this.killNow(game);
      return;
    }
    // 敌对弹命中玩家（原版 Projectile.Damage_EVP :13706-13830）：不消耗弹体，
    // Player.damage 自带 iframes 去重（= 原版 player.immune 门禁）；
    // 实际造成伤害才走 StatusPlayer 的 debuff 授予（:13798-13800）
    if (this.hostile && hitPlayer(this, game, this.damage)) statusPlayer(game, this.projId);
    // 反射后的弹体 friendly 已翻（原版 Damage NPC 需 friendly 门禁，小动物也是 NPC）
    // ——不再伤小动物/敌怪，只走上方 hitPlayer 回打发射者
    if (this.reflected) return;
    // 小动物:一击致死并消耗弹体(原版小动物是 NPC;近战挥砍同语义)
    if (hitCritters(this, game)) { this.killNow(game); return; }
    // 命中敌人（原版 penetrate：箭 1 射中即停；手里剑 4 穿 4 敌，同敌免疫防连击）
    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; id: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] }; reflectsProjectiles?: boolean };
      if (e.dead) continue;
      if (this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y) {
        // 反射源（419 旋刃冲刺等 reflectsProjectiles 置标）：命中前先走反射
        // （原版 Projectile.cs:12050-12060，反射即终止本帧命中迭代 keepIterating=false）
        if (tryReflectProjectile(this, this.projId, e, game)) {
          this.pierce = 1;            // 原版 penetrate=1（NPC.cs:67058）
          this.hitSet.clear();        // 转敌对后命中表作废
          return;
        }
        if (this.hitSet.has(e.id)) continue; // 穿透弹已命中过此敌
        // 暴击：玩家远程基础 4%（原版 ranged 初始 4）。
        // ★鞭 tag（WhipTagEffect.CanRunHitEffects :73-84：MinionShot/SentryShot 弹幕吃
        //   tag——随从/哨兵射出的箭 whipTagShot 置标时 +tagDmg，TagCrit 可暴击）
        let dmg = this.damage;
        let crit = Math.random() < 0.04;
        if (this.whipTagShot) {
          const en2 = e as unknown as { whipTagT?: number; whipTagDmg?: number; whipTagCrit?: number };
          if (en2.whipTagT && en2.whipTagT > 0) {
            dmg += (en2.whipTagDmg ?? 0);
            if (Math.random() * 100 < (en2.whipTagCrit ?? 0)) crit = true;
          }
        }
        if (crit) dmg *= 2;
        if (this.ignite) {
          const en3 = e as unknown as { onFireT?: number };
          en3.onFireT = Math.max(en3.onFireT ?? 0, 5); // AddBuff(24,300)=5s
        }
        if (this.nebulaCb) this.nebulaCb(); // 星云 booster（:12892-12905，伤害>0 门已过）
        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
        e.hurt(dmg, Math.sign(this.vx) * this.knockback, 0, game);
        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, Math.round(dmg), crit, crit ? '#FF8040' : '#FFD060');
        this.hitSet.add(e.id);
        // ---- 吸血链（Projectile.cs:12877-12892 on-hit 段）----
        // 吸血鬼飞刀 304 → vampireHeal（:12879-12882，!moonLeech 门）；
        // 魔法弹 + 幽灵套 ghostHeal → ghostHeal（:12883-12888，canGhostHeal 全 true
        // 近似——雕像产怪 flag 未实装）。ghostHurt（幽灵伤害头 156）未接，登记。
        // numHits = 含本次的命中数（原版 StrikeNPC 先自增，hitSet.size 此时已含本敌）
        if (this.projId === 304) {
          game.applyLifeSteal?.('vampire', Math.round(dmg), this.hitSet.size, this.x, this.y);
        } else if (projectileData(this.projId)?.magic) {
          game.applyLifeSteal?.('ghost', Math.round(dmg), this.hitSet.size, this.x, this.y, true);
        }
        if (--this.pierce <= 0) { this.killNow(game); return; }
      }
    }
  }

  /** aiStyle 14 撞块反弹（Projectile.cs:18314-18327 默认档：法向 ×-0.5，低速归零）。
   *  逐轴回退探测穿透轴并反弹；两轴均无法脱困（嵌入过深）则返回 false 交还消亡。 */
  private bounceOff(game: GameHooks): boolean {
    const st = game.world.store;
    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
      const t0 = Math.floor(px / TILE), t1 = Math.floor(py / TILE);
      return st.inBounds(t0, t1) && st.isSolid(t0, t1);
    };
    let bounced = false;
    // X 轴：回退一步脱离实心 → 穿透轴是 X，反弹并退回
    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2 - this.vx, this.y + this.h / 2)) {
      this.x -= this.vx;
      this.vx *= -0.5;
      bounced = true;
    }
    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - this.vy)) {
      this.y -= this.vy;
      this.vy = this.vy > 1 ? -this.vy * 0.5 : 0;
      bounced = true;
    }
    return bounced;
  }
}

```

		if (spawnSource is EntitySource_Parent entitySource_Parent)

			if (entitySource_Parent.Entity is Projectile projectile)

				bannerIdToRespondTo = projectile.bannerIdToRespondTo;

	private void ApplyStatsFromSource(IEntitySource spawnSource)
	{
		if (spawnSource is EntitySource_ItemUse { Item: var item } entitySource_ItemUse)
		{
			if (ItemID.Sets.UniqueTagEffects[item.type] != null)
			{
				tagEffectType = item.type;
			}
			bonusTagDamage += item.bonusTagDamage;
			armorPenetration += item.armorPenetration;
			if (item.type == 2269 && entitySource_ItemUse.Entity is Player)

				Player player = (Player)entitySource_ItemUse.Entity;
				bonusCritChance += player.revolverCritChanceBonus;

		if (spawnSource is EntitySource_Parent { Entity: Projectile entity })
		{
			tagEffectType = entity.tagEffectType;
			bonusTagDamage += entity.bonusTagDamage;
			armorPenetration += entity.armorPenetration;
			bonusCritChance += entity.bonusCritChance;
		}
	}

	public static void SpawnMoonLordWhipProc(Projectile parent, NPC targetNPC, int procDamage, int aiCounter)
	{
		int num = Main.rand.Next(1, 4);
		num = 1;
		for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
		{
			Rectangle hitbox = targetNPC.Hitbox;
			hitbox.Inflate(30, 16);
			hitbox.Y -= 8;
			Vector2 vector = Main.rand.NextVector2FromRectangle(hitbox);
			Vector2 vector2 = hitbox.Center.ToVector2();
			float num2 = 7f + Main.rand.NextFloat() * 8f;
			Vector2 spinningpoint = (vector2 - vector).SafeNormalize(parent.velocity.SafeNormalize(-Vector2.UnitY)) * num2;
			float num3 = (float)(Main.rand.Next(2) * 2 - 1) * (MathF.PI / 5f + MathF.PI * 4f / 5f * Main.rand.NextFloat());
			num3 *= 0.5f;
			spinningpoint = spinningpoint.RotatedBy(0.7853981852531433);
			int num4 = 1;
			int num5 = 24 * num4;
			int num6 = 16;
			int num7 = num6 * num4;
			vector = vector2;
			for (int j = 0; j < num7; j++)
			{
				vector -= spinningpoint;
				spinningpoint = spinningpoint.RotatedBy((0f - num3) / (float)num5);
			}
			vector += targetNPC.velocity * num6;
			ParticleOrchestrator.RequestParticleSpawn(clientOnly: false, ParticleOrchestraType.MoonLordWhipEye, new ParticleOrchestraSettings
			{
				PositionInWorld = vector,
				MovementVector = -spinningpoint
			}, parent.owner);
			NewProjectile(parent.GetProjectileSource_FromThis(), vector, spinningpoint, 1045, procDamage, 0f, parent.owner, num3, aiCounter);
		}
	}

	private void SpawnConstellationStarsTowards(NPC targetNPC, int calcDamage)
	{
		int num = (int)((float)calcDamage * 0.33f);
		WhipPointsForCollision.Clear();
		FillWhipControlPoints(this, WhipPointsForCollision);
		for (int i = 2; i < WhipPointsForCollision.Count - 1; i += 3)
		{
			Vector2 vector = WhipPointsForCollision[i + 1];
			Vector2 vector2 = WhipPointsForCollision[i];
			Vector2 v = (vector - vector2).RotatedBy(-MathF.PI / 2f * (float)Main.player[owner].direction * Main.player[owner].gravDir);
			NewProjectile(GetProjectileSource_FromThis(), vector2, v.SafeNormalize(Vector2.Zero) * (1f + Main.rand.NextFloatDirection() * 3f), 1039, num, knockBack, owner, targetNPC.whoAmI, 0.02f + 0.09f * Main.rand.NextFloat());
		}
	}

=== WhipTagEffect_Firecracker.cs ===
using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.Items;

public class WhipTagEffect_Firecracker : WhipTagEffect
{
	private static float ProcDamageMultiplier = 1.75f;

	public override void ModifyProcHit(Player owner, Projectile optionalProjectile, NPC npcHit, ref int damageDealt, ref bool crit)
	{
		base.ModifyProcHit(owner, optionalProjectile, npcHit, ref damageDealt, ref crit);
		damageDealt += (int)((float)damageDealt * ProcDamageMultiplier);
	}

	public override void OnProcHit(Player owner, Projectile optionalProjectile, NPC npcHit, int calcDamage)
	{
		CreateExplosion(optionalProjectile, npcHit, (int)((float)calcDamage * ProcDamageMultiplier));
	}

	private static void CreateExplosion(Projectile projectile, NPC npcHit, int procDamage)
	{
		int num = Projectile.NewProjectile(projectile.GetProjectileSource_FromThis(), npcHit.Center, Vector2.Zero, 918, procDamage, 0f, projectile.owner);
		Main.projectile[num].localNPCImmunity[npcHit.whoAmI] = -1;
	}
}
=== WhipTagEffect_Kaleidoscope.cs ===

namespace Terraria.GameContent.Items;

	{
		ParticleOrchestrator.RequestParticleSpawn(clientOnly: false, ParticleOrchestraType.RainbowRodHit, new ParticleOrchestraSettings
		{
			PositionInWorld = optionalProjectile.Center
		});
	}

using System;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.Items;

public class WhipTagEffect_Starcrash : WhipTagEffect
{
	public override void OnProcHit(Player owner, Projectile optionalProjectile, NPC npcHit, int calcDamage)
	{
		SpawnMeteorWhipMeteorOn(optionalProjectile, npcHit, calcDamage);
	}

	private void SpawnMeteorWhipMeteorOn(Projectile projectile, NPC targetNPC, int calcDamage)
	{
		int num = 200;
		int num2 = 600;
		int damage = (int)((float)calcDamage * 1.33f);
		Vector2 vector = new Vector2(-num + Main.rand.Next(num * 2), -num2);
		Vector2 vector2 = targetNPC.Center + vector;
		Vector2 vector3 = vector.SafeNormalize(Vector2.Zero) * -12f;
		int num3 = 8;
		int num4 = 35;
		vector2 = targetNPC.Center + new Vector2(0f, -num3 * num4).RotatedBy(Main.rand.NextFloatDirection() * (MathF.PI * 2f) * 0.125f);
		vector3 = targetNPC.DirectionFrom(vector2) * num3;
		Projectile.NewProjectile(projectile.GetProjectileSource_FromThis(), vector2, vector3, 1037, damage, projectile.knockBack, projectile.owner, Main.rand.Next(3), targetNPC.position.Y);
	}
}
=== WhipTagEffect_DarkHarvest.cs ===
using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;
using Terraria.GameContent.Drawing;
using Terraria.ID;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.Items;

public class WhipTagEffect_DarkHarvest : WhipTagEffect
{
	public override void OnTaggedHit(Player owner, Projectile optionalProjectile, NPC npcHit, int calcDamage)
	{
		SpawnBlackLightning(optionalProjectile, npcHit);
	}

	private void SpawnBlackLightning(Projectile projectile, NPC npcHit)
	{
		int damage = (int)((float)TagDamage * ProjectileID.Sets.SummonTagDamageMultiplier[projectile.type]);
		int num = Projectile.NewProjectile(projectile.GetProjectileSource_FromThis(), npcHit.Center, Vector2.Zero, 916, damage, 0f, projectile.owner);
		Main.projectile[num].localNPCImmunity[npcHit.whoAmI] = -1;
		EmitBlackLightningParticles(npcHit);
	}

	private static void EmitBlackLightningParticles(NPC targetNPC)
	{
		ParticleOrchestrator.RequestParticleSpawn(clientOnly: false, ParticleOrchestraType.BlackLightningHit, new ParticleOrchestraSettings
		{
			PositionInWorld = targetNPC.Center
		});
	}
}

	private void SpawnFlowerExplosionOn(Projectile projectile, NPC targetNPC, int petalDamage)
	{
		float num = Main.rand.NextFloat() * (MathF.PI * 2f);
		float num2 = 3f;
		for (int i = 0; (float)i < num2; i++)
		{
			float num3 = (float)i / num2 * (MathF.PI * 2f) + num;
			float num4 = (float)((targetNPC.width > targetNPC.height) ? targetNPC.width : targetNPC.height) / 8f;
			Vector2 velocity = Vector2.UnitX.RotatedBy(num3).RotatedByRandom(0.39269909262657166) * num4;
			int num5 = Projectile.NewProjectile(projectile.GetProjectileSource_FromThis(), targetNPC.Center, velocity, 1038, petalDamage, 0f, projectile.owner, Main.rand.NextFloat() * -20f);
			Main.projectile[num5].localNPCImmunity[targetNPC.whoAmI] = 30;
		}

Excellent! AI_067_TigerSpecialAttack (:46383-46479):
- Search box: player center, 1600×800 rect.
- num = ownedProjectileCounts[831] + 1 = gems+1 (max pounces = gem count + 1).
- Targets: nearest-to-player enemy (nPC2, last), nearest-to-self (nPC3, first), plus random others with velocity lead (count up to num-1 total in list2 before nPC2 appended).
- Each hop: spawn 818 from current pos to target (±20px X offset), ai[0] = -num4*4 (delay = cumulative ticks ×4, negative = start delay), ai[1] = num6*4 (duration). num6 = min(num3, 4+ceil(dist/50)) clamped min 5; num3 = min(20, 30/list2.Count).
- Tiger teleports to last position, ai[0]=4 (state), ai[1]=num4 (total chain duration).

818 AI_162: ai[0] starts negative (delay, increments by 1 each update while <0 — with extraUpdates=3 that's 4 increments per tick... wait extraUpdates=3 means the projectile updates 4 times per tick). ai[0] += 1/ai[1] each update. Duration in ticks = ai[1]/4 (since 4 updates per tick, ai[0] increases by 4/ai[1] per tick → reaches 1 after ai[1]/4 ticks). With ai[1]=num6*4 → duration num6 ticks. Makes sense.

Pounce is a 132×132 box, damage = tiger damage ×(1.5+0.4n), localNPCHitCooldown = -1 (hit each NPC once — via localNPCImmunity). CatmullRom arc with y = dist*1.5 offset, so it arcs upward (leap).

Also key: when is TigerSpecialAttack triggered? Let me look at the call site in AI_067.
```

The issue is only the aura calling every 3 ticks (effective 12).

Hmm wait, one subtlety: our `ready` when hit sets t = cd, then next tick t=cd>0 → decrement → return false... so hits happen every cd+1 ticks! Vanilla: hit sets immunity=3; next update immunity 3→2 (no hit possible, since != 0 check), 2→1, 1→0, then hit. So immunity 0 after 3 decrements: hit at t0, t1 no (3), t2 no (2), t3 no (1→0)... wait at t3: immunity is 1 >0 → decrement to 0, no hit. At t4: immunity 0 → hit. So hit every 4 ticks? But actually when is the immunity relative to damage: in Damage(), immuneArray[victimIndex] is checked where? 	public static Vector2 GetPlayerArmPosition(Projectile proj, Player theOwner)
	{
		Vector2 vector = OffsetsPlayerOnhand[theOwner.bodyFrame.Y / 56] * 2f;
		if (theOwner.direction != 1)
		{
			vector.X = (float)theOwner.bodyFrame.Width - vector.X;
		}
		if (theOwner.gravDir != 1f)
		{
			vector.Y = (float)theOwner.bodyFrame.Height - vector.Y;
		}
		vector -= new Vector2(theOwner.bodyFrame.Width - theOwner.width, (float)theOwner.bodyFrame.Height - theOwner.BaseHeight) / 2f;
		Vector2 pos = theOwner.MountedCenter - new Vector2(theOwner.width, theOwner.BaseHeight) / 2f + vector;
		if (theOwner.mount.Active && theOwner.mount.Type == 52)
		{
			pos.Y -= theOwner.mount.PlayerOffsetHitbox;
			pos += new Vector2(12 * theOwner.direction, -12f);
		}
		theOwner.ApplyItemPositionOffsetFromMount(ref pos);
		return theOwner.RotatedRelativePoint(pos);
	}

// R6 召唤系统回归：combatWeapon summon 分流 / 随从上限驱逐 / 随从行为（mock 驱动）
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { combatWeapon } from '../src/data/vanillaItemCombat';
import { MinionProj } from '../src/entities/MinionProj';
import { Player } from '../src/entities/Player';
import { Inventory } from '../src/items/Inventory';
import { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';
import type { GameHooks } from '../src/entities/types';

// ---- mock 环境（weapons-entities 同款） ----
const W = 200, H = 120;
function makeWorld() {
  const store = new TileStore(W, H);
  for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) for (let y = 100; y < H; y++) store.setTile(x, y, 1);
  return store;
}
function makeHooks(enemies: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = []) {
  const store = makeWorld();
  const player = new Player(60 * 16, 90 * 16, new Inventory());
  const added: unknown[] = [];
  const hooks: GameHooks = {
    world: { store } as never,
    player: player as never,
    enemies: () => enemies,
    critters: () => [],
    spawnDrop: () => null,
    damagePlayer: () => {},
    addDamageNumber: () => {},
    cutTile: () => {},
    onEnemyKilled: () => {},
    spawnEnemy: () => {},
    spawnParticles: () => {},
    notifyInventoryChanged: () => {},
    playSfx: () => {},
    playSfxFiles: () => {},
    showPickupLabel: () => {},
  };
  // 随从射击出口（MinionProj.fixedUpdate 射弹经 entities.add 入 projectiles 桶）——
  // GameHooks 未声明 entities（生产侧统一以 as unknown 收窄），测试同样旁挂
  (hooks as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown) => void } }).entities =
    { add: (e: unknown) => { added.push(e); } };
  return { hooks, store, player, added };
}

const iid = (key: string) => {
  // 直接构造满足 CombatWeaponItemLike 的形状（vi_ key 反解）
  return { key };
};

describe('combatWeapon summon 分流', () => {
  it('小鬼法杖 2365 → kind=summon 非哨兵；寒霜九头蛇 1572 → 哨兵（ai 53）', () => {
    const imp = combatWeapon(iid('vi_2365_ImpStaff'));
    expect(imp?.kind).toBe('summon');
    expect((imp as { sentry?: boolean }).sentry).toBe(false);
    expect(imp?.damage).toBe(17);
    const hydra = combatWeapon(iid('vi_1572_FrostHydraStaff'));
    expect(hydra?.kind).toBe('summon');
    expect((hydra as { sentry?: boolean }).sentry).toBe(true);
  });
  it('31 件召唤武器全部解析为 summon', () => {
    const ids = [1157, 1309, 1572, 1802, 2364, 2365, 2366, 2535, 2551, 2584, 2621, 2749,
      3249, 3474, 3531, 3569, 3571, 3834, 4269, 4273, 4281, 4607, 4758, 5005, 5069,
      5114, 5119, 5456, 5463, 5663, 5664];
    let ok = 0;
    for (const id of ids) {
      const w = combatWeapon({ key: `vi_${id}_x` });
      if (w?.kind === 'summon') ok++;
    }
    expect(ok).toBe(ids.length);
  });
});

describe('MinionProj 行为', () => {
  it('飞行随从：无目标悬停玩家侧后 → 有目标朝目标移动 → >1300px 瞬移回', () => {
    const { hooks, player } = makeHooks();
    const m = new MinionProj(player.x, player.y, 375, 17, 2, false, 0, false, () => player);
    // 无敌悬停几帧：靠近玩家
    for (let k = 0; k < 60; k++) m.fixedUpdate(1, hooks);
    expect(Math.hypot(m.cx - player.cx, m.cy - player.cy)).toBeLessThan(80);
    // 出现敌人 → 靠近
    const enemy: Record<string, unknown> = {
      id: 1, x: 100 * 16, y: 80 * 16, w: 16, h: 24, dead: false,
      cx: 100 * 16 + 8, cy: 80 * 16 + 12,
      hp: 1000, hurt: (d: number) => { enemy.hp = (enemy.hp as number) - d; return true; },
    };
    const { hooks: h2 } = makeHooks([enemy]);
    const d0 = Math.hypot(enemy.cx as number - m.cx, enemy.cy as number - m.cy);
    for (let k = 0; k < 220; k++) m.fixedUpdate(1, h2); // 640px 距离 / 5px 每帧
    expect(Math.hypot((enemy.cx as number) - m.cx, (enemy.cy as number) - m.cy)).toBeLessThan(d0);
    // 接触造成伤害（hp 下降）
    expect(enemy.hp).toBeLessThan(1000);
    // 远离 >1300 → 瞬移回
    m.x = player.cx + 2000;
    m.fixedUpdate(1, h2);
    expect(Math.hypot(m.cx - player.cx, m.cy - player.cy)).toBeLessThan(100);
  });
  it('哨兵：定点 60t 一发（added 计数）+ 寿命耗尽自灭', () => {
    const enemy = {
      id: 2, x: 80 * 16, y: 90 * 16, w: 16, h: 24, dead: false,
      get cx() { return this.x + 8; }, get cy() { return this.y + 12; },
      hp: 1000, hurt: () => true,
    };
    const { hooks, player } = makeHooks([enemy]);
    const s = new MinionProj(player.x, player.y, 308, 100, 7.5, true, 0, false, () => player);
    // 注入 entities.add 通道
    (hooks as unknown as { entities: unknown }).entities = {
      add: (e: unknown) => { (enemy as unknown as { __shots?: unknown[] }).__shots = [((enemy as unknown as { __shots?: unknown[] }).__shots ?? []), e].flat(); },
    };
    const h = hooks as unknown as GameHooks & { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } };
    for (let k = 0; k < 130; k++) s.fixedUpdate(1, h);
    const shots = (enemy as unknown as { __shots?: unknown[] }).__shots ?? [];
    expect(shots.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
    expect(s.dead).toBe(false);
    s.life = 1;
    s.fixedUpdate(1, h);
    expect(s.dead).toBe(true);
  });
});

fillControlPoints(getActualCollisionPoints: boolean): {x,y}[] {
  const segments = WHIP_SEGMENTS[this.projId] ?? 20;
  const rangeMul = WHIP_RANGE[this.projId] ?? 1;
  const timeToFlyOut = this.anim * this.maxUpdates; // ai[0] counts updates
  let num = this.ai0 / timeToFlyOut;
  if (this.projId === 1028) num = remap(num, 0, 1, 0.3, 0.95);
  const num2 = 0.5, num3 = 1.5;
  const num4 = Math.PI * 10 * (1 - num * num3) * -this.spriteDir / segments;
  let num5 = num * num3, num6 = 0;
  if (num5 > 1) { num6 = (num5 - 1) / num2; num5 = lerp(1, 0, num6); }
  const num7 = this.anim * 2 * num * whipRangeMul;   // useAnimation*2 (num7 :45642)
  const num8 = this.speed * num7 * num5 * rangeMul / segments;  // velocity.Length()=shootSpeed
  const arm = this.armPos; // anchor
  let num10 = -Math.PI/2, num12 = Math.PI/2, num11 = Math.PI/2 + Math.PI/2 * this.spriteDir;
  let v1 = arm, v2 = arm, v3 = arm; // vector, vector3, vector2
  const pts = [arm];
  const num13 = getActualCollisionPoints ? 1 : this.ai1; // ai[1] random per swing
  for (let i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
    const num14 = i / segments;
    const num15 = num4 * num14 * 1 * num13;
    const vector4 = v1 + rot(num10) * num8;
    const vector5 = v3 + rot(num12) * (num8 * 2);
    const vector6 = v2 + rot(num11) * (num8 * 2);
    const num16 = 1 - num5;
    const num17 = 1 - num16 * num16;
    const value = lerp(vector5, vector4, num17 * 0.9 + 0.1);
    const vector7 = lerp(vector6, value, num17 * 0.7 + 0.3);
    const spin = arm + (vector7 - arm) * (1, num3);
    const num18 = num6 * num6;
    const item = rotateAround(spin, arm, this.rotation + 4.712389 * num18 * this.spriteDir);
    pts.push(item);
    num10 += num15; num12 += num15; num11 += num15;
    v1 = vector4; v3 = vector5; v2 = vector6;
  }
  return pts;
}

// :45664\n      const num16 = 1 - num5;       // :45665\n      const num17 = 1 - num16 * num16; // :45666\n      const value = lerpV(vector5, vector4, num17 * 0.9 + 0.1);  // :45667\n      const vector7 = lerpV(vector6, value, num17 * 0.7 + 0.3); // WhipTagEffect TagDuration（WhipTagEffect.cs:15-18）\n        e.whipTagDmg = WHIP_TAG[this.itemId] ?? 0;\n        e.whipTagCrit = WHIP_TAG_CRIT[this.itemId] ?? 0;\n        e.whipTagItem = this.itemId;\n        // proc 启用（TryDoingOnHitEffects :12936-12940 case 913/1031/1032 首命中门；\n        //  :12971-12974 case 1035 无门）——TryEnableProcOnNPC 要求当前激活 tag 效果\n        //  与期望一致（TagEffectState.cs:170-176），本次命中刚写入 → 恒真\n        if (WHIP_PROC_ITEMS.has(this.itemId) && (this.itemId === 5480 || !this.procEnabled)) {\n          this.procEnabled = true;\n          e.whipProcT = 240;\n          e.whipProcItem = this.itemId;\n        }\n        // 5479（proj 1034）星座星：鞭命中即沿曲线撒星（:12966-12970 localAI[0]==0 门\n        //  + SpawnConstellationStarsTowards :13869-13881）——伤 = 本次命中伤×0.33，\n        //  沿鞭每 3 个控制点一颗、垂直曲线方向飞出\n        if (this.projId === 1034 && this.localAI0 === 0) {\n          this.localAI0 = 1;\n          const starDmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg * 0.33));\n          const add = (el: unknown) =>\n            (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (x: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(el, 'projectiles');\n          for (let i = 2; i < pts.length - 1; i += 3) {\n            const a = pts[i], b = pts[i + 1];\n            const dx = b.x - a.x, dy = b.y - a.y;\n            const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n            // 垂直方向（-π/2 旋转）× (1±3) 随机速率（:13875-13878）\n            const s = 1 + (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 3;\n            add(new Arrow(a.x, a.y, -dy / d * s, dx / d * s, starDmg, this.knockback, 1039, null,\n              { grav: 0, life: 240 }), 'projectiles');\n          }\n        }\n        // 鞭敌方直伤 debuff（ApplyWhipDebuffs :11067-11090）：913 火鞭→地狱火 323/240、\n        //  912 酷鞭→霜燃 324/240（两者均为 DoT，本作复用 onFire 4s 近似）、914 荆棘鞭→\n        //  1/5 中毒 180；1033 电鳗 362/240、849 黑收成 310/240 无对应 DoT 字段（标记）\n        const deb = e as unknown as { onFireT?: number; poisonT?: number };\n        if (this.projId === 913 || this.projId === 912) deb.onFireT = Math.max(deb.onFireT ?? 0, 4);\n        if (this.projId === 914 && Math.random() < 0.2) deb.poisonT = Math.max(deb.poisonT ?? 0, 3);\n        // 玩家 buff（tag 链 WhipTagEffect :42-51 与命中链 :12936-12951 两源，见表注释）\n        const tb = WHIP_TAG_BUFF[this.itemId];\n        const hb = WHIP_HIT_BUFF[this.itemId];\n        if (tb && owner) owner.whipBuffs[tb] = 180;\n        if (hb && owner) owner.whipBuffs[hb] = 180;\n        if (WHIP_HIT_SIDEKICK[this.itemId] && owner) this.spawnSidekick(game, owner, this.itemId);\n        game.addDamageNumber(p.x, p.y, dmg, false, '#FFD060');\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 命中附属召唤（:12936-12951）：酷鞭 4911 → 雪花哨兵 917（伤 15，buff 312 维持）；\n   *  麻线鞭 5473 → 小蜘蛛 1036（伤 2，buff 365 维持）——同类在场不重复生成\n   *  （原版 coolWhipBuff/cobWhipBuff flag 在 buff 期内置位） */\n  private spawnSidekick(game: GameHooks, owner: Player, itemId: number): void {\n    const want = WHIP_HIT_SIDEKICK[itemId];\n    if (!want) return;\n    const projs = (game as unknown as { entities: { projectiles: MinionProj[]; add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities;\n    if (projs.projectiles.some((m) => m instanceof MinionProj && !m.dead && m.projId === want.proj)) return;\n    const m = new MinionProj(owner.cx, owner.cy - 30, want.proj, want.dmg, 1.5,\n      false, 0, false, () => (owner.dead ? null : owner));\n    m.noSlot = true;\n    projs.add(m, 'projectiles');\n  }\n\n  draw(r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer): void {\n    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n    const img = projFrameImg(this.projId, 0);\n    // 视觉曲线（带随机相位卷曲 :45653）\n    const pts = this.controlPoints(false);\n    const arm = pts[0];\n    if (!img) {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = '#D8B060';\n      ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.moveTo(arm.x, arm.y);\n      for (const p of pts) ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      return;\n    }\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;\n    // 沿控制点排布贴图段（原版 DrawProj_Whip 以控制点切向切片绘制，此处以\n    // projFrames 帧数对齐切向排布近似）\n    const frames = projFrameCount(this.projId);\n    const segs = frames > 1 ? Math.min(frames, pts.length - 1) : Math.min(pts.length - 1, 10);\n    for (let s = 0; s < segs; s++) {\n      const a = pts[Math.floor((s / segs) * (pts.length - 1))];\n      const b = pts[Math.min(pts.length - 1, Math.floor(((s + 1) / segs) * (pts.length - 1)))];\n      const ang = Math.atan2(b.y - a.y, b.x - a.x);\n      ctx.save();\n      ctx.translate(a.x, a.y);\n      ctx.rotate(ang + Math.PI / 2);\n      if (this.spriteDir < 0) ctx.scale(1, -1);\n      ctx.drawImage(img, -img.width / 2, -img.height / 2);\n      ctx.restore();\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n}\n"
}

Now MinionProj.ts. Changes:

**A. MinionHitCd cadence fix** (:15330 decrement-then-check):
```ts
export class MinionHitCd {
  private map = new Map<number, number>();
  /** Projectile.Update :15330 先衰减后判定 + Damage_PVE :11880 array[i]==0 门 +
   *  命中写入 localNPCHitCooldown（:13153）→ 命中后恰好 cd tick 再命中（cd=3 → 每 3t） */
  ready(id: number, cd: number): boolean {
    const t = this.map.get(id) ?? 0;
    if (t > 1) { this.map.set(id, t - 1); return false; }
    this.map.set(id, cd);
    return true;
  }
}
```

**B. Sentinels (aiStyle 53) 1:1**: replace SENTRY_SHOOT branch:
- init ai[0]=120 (localAI[0]==0 block :27176-27181)
- per tick: gravity+land (velocity.Y += 0.2 cap 16) ✓ existing
- targeting: AI_053_HandleSentryNPCTargeting(:37845) — maxDistance 1000, no LOS (straightLine=false). Existing nearestEnemy(1000) ✓.

CatmullRom(v1,v2,v3,v4,amount): XNA's Vector2.CatmullRom formula:
```
0.5 * (2*v2 + (-v1+v3)*t + (2v1-5v2+4v3-v4)*t² + (-v1+3v2-3v3+v4)*t³)
```
XNA CatmullRom(value1..value4, amount) = 0.5*(2*value2 + (value3-value1)*amount + (2value1-5value2+4value3-value4)*amount² + (3value2-3value2... let me recall exactly:
value = 0.5 * (2*value2 + (value3 - value1) * amount + (2*value1 - 5*value2 + 4*value3 - value4) * amount² + (3*value2... no: (-value1 + 3*value2 - 3*value3 + value4) * amount³). Yes standard.

Note the call: `Vector2.CatmullRom(amount: ..., value1: value, value2: vector, value3: vector2, value4: value2)` → value1=arc point before, value2=start, value3=end, value4=arc point after.

 "new_string": "/** 接触伤害的每敌冷却（原版 localNPCImmunity 1:1）：\n *  - Projectile.Update :15330 每 update 先整体衰减（DecrementLocalImmuneTimeCounters\n *    :15926-15941），再跑碰撞（Damage_PVE :11878-11884 要求 array[i]==0）\n *  - 命中写入 immuneArray[victimIndex] = localNPCHitCooldown（:13152-13154，\n *    同时 targetNPC.immune[owner]=0 —— 不吃玩家的全局受击免疫）\n *  ⇒ 命中后恰好 cd 个 tick 再命中（cd=3 → 每 3t 一跳；旧实现\"先判后减\"多出 1t） */\nexport class MinionHitCd {\n  private map = new Map<number, number>();\n  ready(id: number, cd: number): boolean {\n    const t = this.map.get(id) ?? 0;\n    if (t > 1) { this.map.set(id, t - 1); return false; }\n    this.map.set(id, cd);\n    return true;\n  }\n}"

```ts
private ravenBody(game: GameHooks, p: Player, dmg0: number): void {
  // 同类分离（:27397-27416）
  const projs = (game as ...).entities.projectiles;
  for (const m of projs) { if (m instanceof MinionProj && !m.dead && m !== this && m.projId === 317) {
    if (Math.abs(this.x - m.x) + Math.abs(this.y - m.y) < this.w) {
      this.vx += this.x < m.x ? -0.05 : 0.05;
      this.vy += this.y < m.y ? -0.05 : 0.05;
    }}}
  // 归位门（:27425-27430）
  const far = Math.abs(this.cx - p.cx) + Math.abs(this.cy - p.cy);
  if (far > 500) this.rA0 = 1;
  // 索敌（ai[0]==0 时；:27433-27485：900 曼哈顿 + 视线）
  let tgt: EnemyLike | null = null;
  if (this.rA0 === 0) {
    let best = 900;
    for (const e of game.enemies()) {
      const en = e as EnemyLike;
      if (en.dead) continue;
      const d = Math.abs(this.cx - en.cx) + Math.abs(this.cy - en.cy);
      if (d < best && canHit(game.world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, en.x, en.y, en.w, en.h)) { best = d; tgt = en; }
    }
  }
  if (!tgt) {
    // 跟随玩家（:27499-27545）：速 8（ai[0]==1 → 12；317 且距>100 → 12/15）
    const dx = p.cx - this.cx, dy = p.cy - 60 - this.cy;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (dist < 100 && this.rA0 === 1 && !solidAt(self)) this.rA0 = 0;  // :27512-27517
    if (dist > 2000) { this.x = p.cx - this.w/2; this.y = p.cy - this.w/2; }  // :27519-27522
    let spd = this.rA0 === 1 ? 12 : 8;
    if (dist > 100) spd = this.rA0 === 1 ? 15 : 12;   // :27524-27531（317 专属）
    if (dist > 70) {
      this.vx = (this.vx * 20 + dx / dist * spd) / 21;
      this.vy = (this.vy * 20 + dy / dist * spd) / 21;
    } else {
      if (this.vx === 0 && this.vy === 0) { this.vx = -0.15; this.vy = -0.05; }
      this.vx *= 1.01; this.vy *= 1.01;
    }
    this.rot = this.vx * 0.05;
    // 帧 0-3（:27546-27554）—— draw() 走 rFrame
    this.rFrameZone = 0;
    if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.2) this.facing = -(Math.sign(this.vx));   // spriteDirection = -direction
    this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
    return;  // friendly=false：无接触伤
  }
  // 扑击循环（:27557-27590）
  if (this.rA1 === -1) this.rA1 = 17;
  if (this.rA1 > 0) this.rA1--;
  if (this.rA1 === 0) {
    // 冲刺（friendly=true，接触伤）
    const dx = tgt.cx - this.cx, dy = tgt.cy - this.cy;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    const spd = dist < 100 ? 10 : 16;
    this.vx = (this.vx * 14 + dx / dist * spd) / 15;
    this.vy = (this.vy * 14 + dy / dist * spd) / 15;
    this.charging = true;
  } else {
    this.charging = false;
    if (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy) < 10) { this.vx *= 1.05; this.vy *= 1.05; }
  }
  this.rot = this.vx * 0.05;
  this.rFrameZone = 1;  // 帧 4-7
  if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.2) this.facing = -Math.sign(this.vx);
  this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
  if (this.charging) this.contact(game, dmg0, 10);   // localNPCHitCooldown=10（:3836）
  // 命中重置扑击计时（case 317 :12777-12780 ai[1]=-1）
  ...
}

relX : relX / dl * maxAmt; // MoveTowards(Zero, rel, maxAmt)\n        const desY = dl <= maxAmt ? relY : relY / dl * maxAmt;\n        // :42224-42225 velocity.MoveTowards(desired, 32)\n        const vl = Math.hypot(desX - this.vx, desY - this.vy);\n        if (vl > 32) { this.vx += (desX - this.vx) / vl * 32; this.vy += (desY - this.vy) / vl * 32; }\n        else { this.vx = desX; this.vy = desY; }\n        this.facing = dirX; // :42226\n        this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;\n        // 每 4t 重锁 500 内最近敌（:42227-42238）\n        if (++this.gRetarget >= 4) {\n          this.gRetarget = 0;\n          const nt = this.nearestEnemy(game, 500);\n          if (nt) this.gA1 = nt.id;\n        }\n        // 攻击帧 12-19（12/13 快速、14 后保持 :42173-42191）\n        if (this.gFrame < 12) this.gFrame = 12;\n        const rate = this.gFrame === 12 || this.gFrame === 13 ? 8 : 3;\n        if (++this.gFc >= rate) { this.gFc = 0; if (++this.gFrame >= 19) this.gFrame = 14; }\n        // 拳盒命中（:14294-14300：状态 2 才有碰撞，80×40 @ Center+direction×40）\n        for (const ent of game.enemies()) {\n          const en = ent as never as EnemyLike & import('./WhipTag').WhipTagged;\n          if (en.dead) continue;\n          const bx = this.cx + this.facing * 40 - 40, by = this.cy - 20;\n          if (en.x + en.w <= bx || en.x >= bx + 80 || en.y + en.h <= by || en.y >= by + 40) continue;\n          if (!this.hitCd.ready(en.id, 5)) continue; // localNPCHitCooldown=5（:6433）\n          const res = resolveWhipTagHit(en, dmg0, game, add, SUMMON_TAG_MUL[623] ?? 1, en.cx, en.cy);\n          en.hurt(res.dmg, Math.sign(en.cx - this.cx) * this.knockback, -1, game);\n          this.attackFlash = 24;\n        }\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n    // ---- 状态 0：贴身跟随 + 索敌 ----\n    this.x += (restX - this.cx) * 0.05; // :42022 Lerp(Center, vector, 0.05)\n    this.y += (restY - this.cy) * 0.05;\n    this.vx *= 0.5; this.vy *= 0.5;     // :42023\n    this.facing = p.facing;             // :42024\n    if (++this.gFc >= 9) { this.gFc = 0; if (++this.gFrame >= 8) this.gFrame = 0; } // :42025-42032\n    let tgt: EnemyLike | null = null;\n    if (Math.abs(this.cx - restX) <= 170) tgt = this.nearestEnemy(game, 500); // :42036-42043（num+20 门 / num2=500）\n    if (tgt) {\n      const dirX = tgt.cx > this.cx ? 1 : -1;\n      // X leash（:42053-42056）：锚内或反向 且 20<|Δx|<150-锚偏+100 → 步进 0.1，否则衰减\n      if ((num6 < 150 || (restX - this.cx) * dirX < 0) && num7 > 20 && num7 < 150 - num6 + 100) {\n        this.vx += 0.1 * dirX;\n      } else this.vx *= 0.7;\n      // Y leash（:42061-42064，num4=100）\n      if ((num8 < 100 || (restY - (this.y + this.h)) * dirY < 0) && num9 > 10 && num9 < 100 - num8 + 10) {\n        this.vy += 0.1 * dirY;\n      } else this.vy *= 0.7;\n      this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;\n      if (num7 < 500) { this.gA0 = 2; this.gA1 = tgt.id; } // :42069-42073（num3=500 → 冲刺）\n    }\n  }"

-0.05 : 0.05;\n        this.vy += this.y < m.y ? -0.05 : 0.05;\n      }\n    }\n    // 归位门（:27425-27430：曼哈顿 > num440=500 → ai[0]=1）\n    if (Math.abs(this.cx - p.cx) + Math.abs(this.cy - p.cy) > 500) this.rA0 = 1;\n    // 索敌（仅 ai[0]==0：num439=900 曼哈顿 + CanHit 视线，:27433-27485）\n    let tgt: EnemyLike | null = null;\n    if (this.rA0 === 0) {\n      let best = 900;\n      for (const e of game.enemies()) {\n        const en = e as EnemyLike;\n        if (en.dead) continue;\n        const d = Math.abs(this.cx - en.cx) + Math.abs(this.cy - en.cy);\n        if (d < best && canHit(game.world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, en.x, en.y, en.w, en.h)) {\n          best = d; tgt = en;\n        }\n      }\n    } else {\n      this.onGround = false; // tileCollide=false（:27498）\n    }\n    if (!tgt) {\n      // 跟随玩家上方 60px（:27501-27545）\n      const dx = p.cx - this.cx, dy = p.cy - 60 - this.cy;\n      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n      // :27512-27517 距<100 且归位态且无遮挡 → 回攻击态\n      if (dist < 100 && this.rA0 === 1) this.rA0 = 0;\n      if (dist > 2000) { this.x = p.cx - this.w / 2; this.y = p.cy - this.w / 2; } // :27519-27522\n      let spd = this.rA0 === 1 ? 12 : 8;              // :27501-27503\n      if (dist > 100) spd = this.rA0 === 1 ? 15 : 12; // :27524-27531（317 专属提速）\n      if (dist > 70) {\n        this.vx = (this.vx * 20 + (dx / dist) * spd) / 21; // :27533-27535\n        this.vy = (this.vy * 20 + (dy / dist) * spd) / 21;\n      } else {\n        if (this.vx === 0 && this.vy === 0) { this.vx = -0.15; this.vy = -0.05; } // :27537-27539\n        this.vx *= 1.01; this.vy *= 1.01;                                            // :27540\n      }\n      this.rot = this.vx * 0.05; // :27542\n      this.ravenZone = 0;        // 帧 0-3（:27543-27549）\n      if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.2) this.facing = -Math.sign(this.vx); // :27551-27553（spriteDirection=-direction）\n      this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;\n      return; // friendly=false（:27544）——跟随期无接触伤\n    }\n    // 扑击循环（:27557-27590）\n    if (this.rA1 === -1) this.rA1 = 17; // :27557-27559\n    if (this.rA1 > 0) this.rA1--;\n    let charging = false;\n    if (this.rA1 === 0) {\n      // 冲刺（friendly=true :27568-27580）：速 16，距<100 → 10；velocity=(v×14+dir)/15\n      const dx = tgt.cx - this.cx, dy = tgt.cy - this.cy;\n      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n      const spd = dist < 100 ? 10 : 16;\n      this.vx = (this.vx * 14 + (dx / dist) * spd) / 15;\n      this.vy = (this.vy * 14 + (dy / dist) * spd) / 15;\n      charging = true;\n    } else if (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy) < 10) {\n      this.vx *= 1.05; this.vy *= 1.05; // :27582-27585\n    }\n    this.rot = this.vx * 0.05; // :27587\n    this.ravenZone = 1;        // 帧 4-7（:27588-27594）\n    if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.2) this.facing = -Math.sign(this.vx);\n    this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;\n    if (charging && this.contact(game, dmg0, 10)) this.rA1 = -1; // 命中重置（:12777-12780）\n  }\n\n"

 "new_string": "  /** 接触伤害通用（pad=判定盒外扩）；鞭 tag + TagCrit + proc 统一走 resolveWhipTagHit\n   *  （TagEffectState.ModifyHit :216-228 / OnHit :230-243，含黑收成 OnTaggedHit 与\n   *  火鞭/星陨/花瓣/月主弹 proc 首跳）。返回是否命中过（乌鸦命中重置扑击计时） */\n  private contact(game: GameHooks, dmg: number, cd: number, pad = 0): boolean {\n    const add = (e: unknown, b: string) =>\n      (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (x: unknown, y: string) => void } }).entities.add(e, b);\n    let anyHit = false;\n    for (const e of game.enemies()) {\n      const en = e as never as EnemyLike & import('./WhipTag').WhipTagged & {\n        hurtFx?: (d: number) => void;\n      };\n      if (en.dead) continue;\n      if (!(this.x - pad < en.x + en.w && this.x + this.w + pad > en.x\n        && this.y - pad < en.y + en.h && this.y + this.h + pad > en.y)) continue;\n      if (!this.hitCd.ready(en.id, cd)) continue;\n      // 黑收成 OnTaggedHit 直伤通道（916 等价）\n      en.hurtFx = (d: number) => { en.hurt(d, Math.sign(en.cx - this.cx), -1, game); };\n      const res = resolveWhipTagHit(en, dmg, game,\n        (el) => add(el, 'projectiles'), SUMMON_TAG_MUL[this.projId] ?? 1, en.cx, en.cy);\n      en.hurt(res.dmg, Math.sign(en.cx - this.cx) * this.knockback * 0.5, -1, game);\n      this.attackFlash = 24;\n      anyHit = true;\n    }\n    return anyHit;\n  }"

 "new_string": "  /** 接触伤害通用（pad=判定盒外扩）；鞭 tag + TagCrit + proc 统一走 resolveWhipTagHit\n   *  （TagEffectState.ModifyHit :216-228 / OnHit :230-243，含黑收成 OnTaggedHit 与\n   *  火鞭/星陨/花瓣/月主弹 proc 首跳）。返回是否命中过（乌鸦命中重置扑击计时） */\n  private contact(game: GameHooks, dmg: number, cd: number, pad = 0): boolean {\n    const add = (e: unknown, b: string) =>\n      (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (x: unknown, y: string) => void } }).entities.add(e, b);\n    let anyHit = false;\n    for (const e of game.enemies()) {\n      const en = e as never as EnemyLike & import('./WhipTag').WhipTagged & {\n        hurtFx?: (d: number) => void;\n      };\n      if (en.dead) continue;\n      if (!(this.x - pad < en.x + en.w && this.x + this.w + pad > en.x\n        && this.y - pad < en.y + en.h && this.y + this.h + pad > en.y)) continue;\n      if (!this.hitCd.ready(en.id, cd)) continue;\n      // 黑收成 OnTaggedHit 直伤通道（916 等价）\n      en.hurtFx = (d: number) => { en.hurt(d, Math.sign(en.cx - this.cx), -1, game); };\n      const res = resolveWhipTagHit(en, dmg, game,\n        (el) => add(el, 'projectiles'), SUMMON_TAG_MUL[this.projId] ?? 1, en.cx, en.cy);\n      en.hurt(res.dmg, Math.sign(en.cx - this.cx) * this.knockback * 0.5, -1, game);\n      this.attackFlash = 24;\n      anyHit = true;\n    }\n    return anyHit;\n  }"
}

 "new_string": "/** 敌人最小形状（鸭子类型，避免与 Enemy 循环依赖） */\ninterface EnemyLike {\n  cx: number; cy: number; x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; id: number;\n  dead: boolean;\n  hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean;\n}\n\n/** Utils.Remap（AI_120 :42222 用；value 截断映射 [from1..to1] → [from2..to2]） */\nconst remap01 = (value: number, from1: number, to1: number, from2: number, to2: number): number => {\n  if (value <= from1) return from2;\n  if (value >= to1) return to2;\n  return from2 + (to2 - from2) * (value - from1) / (to1 - from1);\n};\n\n/** XNA Vector2.CatmullRom（AI_162 :44091 样条插值） */\nconst catmullRom = (\n  v1: { x: number; y: number }, v2: { x: number; y: number },\n  v3: { x: number; y: number }, v4: { x: number; y: number }, t: number,\n): { x: number; y: number } => {\n  const t2 = t * t, t3 = t2 * t;\n  return {\n    x: 0.5 * (2 * v2.x + (-v1.x + v3.x) * t + (2 * v1.x - 5 * v2.x + 4 * v3.x - v4.x) * t2 + (-v1.x + 3 * v2.x - 3 * v3.x + v4.x) * t3),\n    y: 0.5 * (2 * v2.y + (-v1.y + v3.y) * t + (2 * v1.y - 5 * v2.y + 4 * v3.y - v4.y) * t2 + (-v1.y + 3 * v2.y - 3 * v3.y + v4.y) * t3),\n  };\n};"
